t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}u_{2}
either happens in
\mathtt{F}
,
\mathtt{L}
,

s_{1}
or
s_{2}
.

\mathtt{F}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\mathtt{F}^{\prime}

with
u_{2}=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
:
We set
s:=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>

which concludes this case since using

Lemma

29
:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\end{array}

\mathtt{L}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\mathtt{L}^{\prime}
with

u_{2}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
:
We set
s:=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>

which concludes this case since using

Lemma

29
:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\end{array}

s_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}s^{\prime}_{1}
with
u_{2}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>
:
We set
s:=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>

which concludes this case since:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s^{\prime}_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\end{array}

s_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}s^{\prime}_{2}
with
u_{2}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>
:
We set
s:=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}]\right>\right>

which concludes this case since:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<\lambda x.s_{1}\right>s^{\prime}_{2}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}[x\backslash s^{\prime}_{2}]\right>\right>\end{array}

\mathcal{R}_{1}={\tt d}\oc
: By definition, there exist

\mathtt{F}
,
\mathtt{L}
,
s\in\Lambda_{\oc}

such that
t=\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>
and
u_{1}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s\right>\right>
. Since

u_{1}\neq u_{2}
, then the step
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}u_{2}
either happens in
\mathtt{F}
,
\mathtt{L}

or
s
.

\mathtt{F}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\mathtt{F}^{\prime}

with
u_{2}=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>
: We set
s:=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s\right>\right>
which concludes
this case since using

Lemma

29
:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s\right>\right>\end{array}

\mathtt{L}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\mathtt{L}^{\prime}
with

u_{2}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>
:
We set
s:=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s\right>\right>
which
concludes this case since using

Lemma

29
:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s\right>\right>\end{array}

s\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}s^{\prime}
with
u_{2}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}\right>\right)\right>
: We set

s:=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s^{\prime}\right>\right>
which
concludes this case since:

\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s\right>\right)\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s\right>\right>\\[2.84544pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}&&\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{2}\right>}\\[2.84544pt]
\mathtt{F}\!\left<{\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s^{\prime}\right>\right)\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<\mathcal{R}_{1}\right>}&\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{L}\!\left<s^{\prime}\right>\right>\end{array}

∎

Corollary 31
.

The reduction
\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt dB}\right>}\cup\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt d}\oc\right>}
is confluent.

Proof.

Immediate consequence of
Lemma

30
.
∎

A.2
Confluence of
\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}

A.2.1
Local Confluence of
\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}

Definition 32
.

Let us now extend the
\mathrm{F}
-reduction
relation to contexts. We define the context reduction relation

\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}
to be the union of the

\mathtt{F}
-closures of the following relations

{\tt s}\oc_{1}
and
{\tt s}\oc_{2}
:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{F}]\right>]&\mapsto_{{\tt s}\oc_{1}}&\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{F}]\right>\\
t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc\mathtt{F}\right>]&\mapsto_{{\tt s}\oc_{2}}&\mathtt{L}\!\left<t\{x\backslash\mathtt{F}\}\right>\end{array}

Lemma 33
.

Let
\mathtt{F}^{1},\mathtt{F}^{2}

be two contexts such that
\mathtt{F}^{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\mathtt{F}^{2}
, then
for any term
t\in\Lambda_{\oc}
, one has that

\mathtt{F}^{1}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\mathtt{F}^{2}\!\left<t\right>
.

Proof.

By definition, there exist three contexts
\mathtt{F},\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}
and
\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}

such that
\mathtt{F}^{1}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}\right>
,

\mathtt{F}^{2}=\mathtt{F}\!\left<\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}\right>
and

\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}\mapsto_{\mathcal{R}}\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}
for some
\mathcal{R}\in\{{\tt s}\oc_{1},{\tt s}\oc_{2}\}
. We distinguish two cases:

\bullet

\mathcal{R}={\tt s}\oc_{1}
: Then

\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}=t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{F}]\right>]
and

\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}=\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{F}]\right>
.
Thus
\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>=t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\oc u\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{F}\!\left<t\right>]\right>]
and

\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>=\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{F}\!\left<t\right>]\right>
.
Therefore
\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>\mapsto_{{\tt s}\oc}\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>
hence

\mathtt{F}^{1}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\mathtt{F}^{2}\!\left<t\right>
.

\bullet

\mathcal{R}={\tt s}\oc_{2}
: Then

\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}=t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc\mathtt{F}\right>]
and

\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<t\{x\backslash\mathtt{F}\}\right>
. Thus

\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>=t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc\mathtt{F}\!\left<t\right>\right>]
and

\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>=\mathtt{L}\!\left<t\{x\backslash\mathtt{F}\!\left<t\right>\}\right>
. Therefore

\mathtt{F}^{1^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>\mapsto_{{\tt s}\oc}\mathtt{F}^{2^{\prime}}\!\left<t\right>
hence

\mathtt{F}^{1}\!\left<t\right>\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\mathtt{F}^{2}\!\left<t\right>
.
∎

Remark 34
.

We also trivially extend the notion of substitution to

\mathtt{F}
-contexts by setting
\diamond\{x\backslash t\}:=\diamond
.

Lemma 35
.

The relation
\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}
is locally confluent.

Proof.

Let
t,u_{1},u_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{1}
,
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{2}
and
u_{1}\neq u_{2}
. We reason by induction on
t
:

t=x
: Impossible since it contradicts
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{1}
.

t=\lambda x.t^{\prime}
: Since
t
cannot be a

{\tt s}\oc
-redex, then necessarily

u_{1}=\lambda x.t^{\prime}_{1}
and
u_{2}=\lambda x.t^{\prime}_{2}
with
t^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime}_{1}
and
t^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime}_{2}
. By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, there exists

s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s^{\prime}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s^{\prime}
. We set
s:=\lambda x.s^{\prime}

concluding this case since by contextual closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\lambda x.t^{\prime}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\lambda x.t^{\prime}_{1}\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
\lambda x.t^{\prime}_{2}&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\lambda x.s^{\prime}\end{array}

t=t_{1}t_{2}
: Since
t
cannot be a

{\tt s}\oc
-redex, four different
cases can be distinguished:

u_{1}=u^{\prime}_{1}t_{2}
,
u_{2}=u^{\prime}_{2}t_{2}

with
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, there
exists
s_{1}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
u^{\prime}_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{1}
and
u^{\prime}_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{1}
. We set
s:=s_{1}t_{2}
concluding this case since by contextual
closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}t_{2}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}t_{2}\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
u^{\prime}_{2}t_{2}&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&s_{1}t_{2}\end{array}

u_{1}=u^{\prime}_{1}t_{2}
,
u_{2}=t_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}

with
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: We set
s:=u^{\prime}_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}
concluding this case since by contextual
closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}t_{2}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}t_{2}\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
t_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}\end{array}

u_{1}=t_{1}u^{\prime}_{1}
,
u_{2}=u^{\prime}_{1}t_{2}

with
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: We set
s:=u^{\prime}_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}
concluding this case since by contextual
closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}t_{2}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
u^{\prime}_{1}t_{2}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}\end{array}

u_{1}=t_{1}u^{\prime}_{1}
,
u_{2}=t_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}

with
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: By
i.h.
on
t_{2}
, there
exists
s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{2}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{2}
. We set
s:=t_{1}s_{2}
concluding this case since by contextual
closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}t_{2}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}u^{\prime}_{1}\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
t_{1}u^{\prime}_{2}&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}s^{\prime}\end{array}

t=t_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]
: Notice that
t
can be a

{\tt s}\oc
-redex but since
u_{1}\neq u_{2}
, three cases have to be distinguished:

t
is the redex reduced in
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{1}
: Then
t_{2}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>
and
u_{1}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>
. We
distinguish two cases:

\bullet

u_{2}=u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]
and
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
: We set
s=\mathtt{L}\!\left<u^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>
. By induction on
t_{1}
,
one has that
t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}
thus by
contextual closure, one has that:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\\[5.69046pt]
\hskip 21.33955pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\hskip 21.33955pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
\mathtt{L}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\mathtt{L}\!\left<u^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>\end{array}

\bullet

u_{2}=t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]
and
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: Since
t_{2}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>
, three cases can be distinguished:

\cdot

\mathtt{L}=\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}[y\backslash\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>
and
u^{\prime}_{2}=\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\right>\right>
: We set
s^{\prime}:=\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\}\right>\right>\right>
.
By induction on
\mathtt{L}_{3}
, one has that

\mathtt{L}_{3}\!\left<t^{\prime}_{2}\right>\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}=\mathtt{L}_{3}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>
.
By
\alpha
-conversion
,
y\notin{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and therefore, by induction on
t_{1}
,

t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}=t_{1}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\}
thus:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\right>\right>\right>]\\[5.69046pt]
\hskip 56.9055pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\hskip 56.9055pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>[y\backslash\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\right>]\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\}\right>\right>\right>\\
&&=\mathtt{L}_{1}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{2}\!\left<\mathtt{L}_{3}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\!\left<t_{1}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\{y\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\}\right>\right>\right>\end{array}

\cdot

u^{\prime}_{2}=\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>
and

\mathtt{L}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\mathtt{L}^{\prime}
:
We set
s^{\prime}:=\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>
which
concludes this case since using

Lemma

33
,
one has:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>]\\[5.69046pt]
\hskip 21.33955pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\hskip 21.33955pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
\mathtt{L}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>\end{array}

\cdot

u^{\prime}_{2}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\right>
and
t^{\prime}_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime\prime}_{2}
: We set
s^{\prime}:=\mathtt{L}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\right>
. By induction on

t_{1}
, one has that
t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}

thus:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime}_{2}\right>]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\right>]\\[5.69046pt]
\hskip 21.33955pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\hskip 21.33955pt\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
\mathtt{L}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime}_{2}\}\right>&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\mathtt{L}\!\left<t_{1}\{x\backslash t^{\prime\prime}_{2}\}\right>\end{array}

t
is the redex reduced in
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{2}
: Same as the previous case.

t
is not the redex reduced in
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{1}
nor
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u_{2}
: We distinguish four cases:

\bullet

u_{1}=u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]
,
u_{2}=u^{\prime}_{2}[x\backslash t_{2}]
with
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, there exists
s_{1}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that

u^{\prime}_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{1}
and
u^{\prime}_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{1}
. We set
s:=s_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]
concluding this case since by contextual
closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\;\;\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
u^{\prime}_{2}[x\backslash t_{2}]&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&s_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]\end{array}

\bullet

u_{1}=u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]
,
u_{2}=t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]
with
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: We set

s:=u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]
concluding this case since by
contextual closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]\end{array}

\bullet

u_{1}=t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{1}]
,
u_{2}=u^{\prime}_{1}xt_{2}
with
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{1}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: We set

s:=u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]
concluding this case since by
contextual closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&u^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]\end{array}

\bullet

u_{1}=t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{1}]
,
u_{2}=t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]
with
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{1}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u^{\prime}_{2}
: By
i.h.
on
t_{2}
, there exists
s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that

t_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{2}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s_{2}
. We set
s:=t_{1}[x\backslash s_{2}]
concluding this case since by contextual
closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}t_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}]&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{1}]\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\;\;\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
t_{1}[x\backslash u^{\prime}_{2}]&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&t_{1}[x\backslash s^{\prime}]\end{array}

t={\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right)
: Since
t
cannot be a

{\tt s}\oc
-redex, then necessarily

u_{1}={\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}_{1}\right)
and
u_{2}={\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}_{2}\right)
with
t^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime}_{1}
and
t^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime}_{2}
. By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, there exists

s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s^{\prime}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s^{\prime}
. We set
s:={\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}\right)

concluding this case since by contextual closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}{\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right)&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&{\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}_{1}\right)\\[5.69046pt]
\;\;\;\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\;\;\;\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
{\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}_{2}\right)&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&{\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}\right)\end{array}

t=\oc t^{\prime}
: Since
t
cannot be a

{\tt s}\oc
-redex, then necessarily

u_{1}=\oc t^{\prime}_{1}
and
u_{2}=\oc t^{\prime}_{2}
with
t^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime}_{1}
and
t^{\prime}\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}t^{\prime}_{2}
. By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, there exists

s^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t_{1}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s^{\prime}
and
t_{2}\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}s^{\prime}
. We set
s:=\oc s^{\prime}
concluding
this case since by contextual closure:

\begin{array}[]{lll}\oc t^{\prime}&\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\oc t^{\prime}_{1}\\[5.69046pt]
\downarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&&\downarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}\\[5.69046pt]
\oc t^{\prime}_{2}&\rightarrow^{*}_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}&\oc s^{\prime}\end{array}

∎

A.2.2
Termination of
\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}

We use the measure defined in
[
8
]
for a simpler framework.

Definition 36
.

The
potential multiplicity

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)
of the
variable
x
in the term
t
is a natural number defined as
follows: if
x\notin{\tt fv}(t)
, then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)=0

and otherwise (where
y\neq x
by
\alpha
-conversion):

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!x}(x)&\coloneqq&1\\
{\tt P}_{\!x}(\lambda y.t)&\coloneqq&{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\\
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}t_{2})&\coloneqq&{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\\
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}[y\backslash t_{2}])&\coloneqq&{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\\
{\tt P}_{\!x}({\tt der}\!\left(t\right))&\coloneqq&{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\\
{\tt P}_{\!x}(\oc t)&\coloneqq&{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\end{array}

The potential multiplicity of
x
in
t
is the number of free
occurrences of
x
in the
unfolding
of
t
, except that for
subterms of the form
s[y\backslash u]
with
y\notin{\tt fv}(s)
the
potential multiplicity of
x
in
u
is counted as if there were
1

free occurrence of
y
in
s
.

By exploiting potential multiplicities we can define a measure of the
global degree of sharing of a given term, and use this measure to
prove that the
\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}
reduction terminates. We
consider multisets of integers. We use
\left[\,\right]
to denote the
empty multiset,
\uplus
to denote multiset union, and
\preceq

(resp.
\prec
) for the standard order (resp. strict order) on
multisets. Given
n,k\in\mathbb{N}
and a finite multiset
M=\left[i_{1},\dots,i_{k}\right]
over natural numbers,
n\cdot M
denotes
the multiset
\left[n\cdot i_{1},\dots,n\cdot i_{k}\right]
(in particular,
n\cdot M=\left[\,\right]
if
M=\left[\,\right]
).

Definition 37
.

The
multiset measure

{\tt m}(t)
of
t\in\Lambda_{\oc}
is a finite multiset over
\mathbb{N}
defined by:

\displaystyle{\tt m}(y)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\,\right]

\displaystyle{\tt m}(\lambda x.t)

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\tt m}(t)

\displaystyle{\tt m}(t_{1}t_{2})

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\tt m}(t_{1})\uplus{\tt m}(t_{2})

\displaystyle{\tt m}(t_{1}[x\backslash t_{2}])

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(t_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(t_{2})

\displaystyle{\tt m}({\tt der}\!\left(t\right))

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\tt m}(t)

\displaystyle{\tt m}(\oc t)

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\tt m}(t)

Lemma 38
.

Let
t,u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
. For any variables
x,y
such that
x\neq y
and
y\notin{\tt fv}(u)
, one has that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)={\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})
.

Proof.

Since
y\notin{\tt fv}(u)
, one deduces that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(u)=0
. We proceed by induction on
t
:

t=z
: We distinguish two cases:

z=x
: Thus
t\{x\backslash u\}=u
and since
x\neq y
then
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)=0
hence

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t)
.

z\neq x
: Thus
t\{x\backslash u\}=z=t
hence

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t)
.

t=\lambda z.t^{\prime}
: We can suppose without loss of
generality that
z\notin{\tt fv}(u)\cup\{x\}
, thus

t\{x\backslash u\}=\lambda z.(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})
. By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, one has
that
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime})={\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rclcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\lambda z.t^{\prime})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{=}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\lambda z.(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}))&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})\end{array}

t=t_{1}t_{2}
: By
i.h.

t_{1}
and
t_{2}
, one has
that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})
and

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})
thus

\begin{array}[]{rclcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}t_{2})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{=}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}((t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}))&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})\end{array}

t=t_{1}[z\backslash t_{2}]
: We can suppose without loss of
generality that
z\notin{\tt fv}(u)\cup\{x\}
, thus

t\{x\backslash u\}=(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}]
. By
i.h.

t_{1}
and
t_{2}
, one has that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})
,
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})
and
{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1})={\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})
. Thus

\begin{array}[]{rclcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[z\backslash t_{2}])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\times{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{=}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}))\times{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}])&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})\end{array}

t={\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right)
: By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime})={\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rclcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}({\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{=}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}({\tt der}\!\left((t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\right))&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})\end{array}

t=\oc t^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime})={\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rclcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc t^{\prime})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{=}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}))&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})\end{array}

∎

Lemma 39
.

Let
t,u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
be terms, then

{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>)\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc u\right>])
.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathtt{L}
:

\mathtt{L}=\diamond
: We proceed by induction on
t
. If
y\notin{\tt fv}(t\{x\backslash u\})
, then
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})=0\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])
.
Let us suppose
y\in{\tt fv}(t\{x\backslash u\})
. Cases:

t=z
: We distinguish two subcases:

\bullet

z=x
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=u
thus

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(u)\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(u)={\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)={\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])
.

\bullet

z\neq x
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=t
thus

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t)\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)={\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])
.

t=\lambda z.t^{\prime}
: We can suppose without loss of generality that
z\notin{\tt fv}(u)\cup\{x\}
, so
t\{x\backslash u\}=\lambda z.(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})
. By
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
,

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\lambda z.(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\lambda z.t^{\prime})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(\lambda z.t^{\prime}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

t=t_{1}t_{2}
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})
and by
i.h.
on
t_{1}

and
t_{2}
, one has that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
and

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])
. We distinguish four
cases:

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=t
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)=0
, thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)\\
&\leq&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\geq 1
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})=0
. By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
and

{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}((t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&=&\left({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\right)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})=0
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\geq 1
. By
i.h.
on
t_{2}
, one has
that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}((t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&\left({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\right)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\geq 1
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\geq 1
. By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, one has
that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
and

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}((t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
&=&\left({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\right)+\left({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\right)\\
&=&\left({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\right)+\left(\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\right)\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

t=t_{1}[z\backslash t_{2}]
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}]
and by
i.h.
on
t_{1}

and
t_{2}
, one has that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
and

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])
. We distinguish four
cases:

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=t
, thus:

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(t)\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)={\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\geq 1
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})=0
. By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
and

{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Moreover, by

\alpha
-conversion
z\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
and
z\notin{\tt fv}(u)
thus
{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{2})=0
and

{\tt P}_{\!z}(\oc u)=0
. Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}[z\backslash t_{2}])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u]))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!z}(\oc u))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})=0
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\geq 1
. By
i.h.
on
t_{2}
, one has
that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Moreover, by

\alpha
-conversion
z\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
thus

{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{2})=0
. Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&\leq&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(t_{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(t_{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\geq 1
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\geq 1
. By
i.h.
on
t_{1}
, one has
that
{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])
and

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Moreover, by

\alpha
-conversion
z\notin{\tt fv}(t_{2})
and
z\notin{\tt fv}(\oc u)
thus
{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{2})=0
and

{\tt P}_{\!z}(\oc u)=0
. Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}[z\backslash t_{2}\{x\backslash u\}])\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}\{x\backslash u\}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}\{x\backslash u\})\\
\quad\leq\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u])+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}[x\backslash\oc u]))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1})\\
\qquad\quad+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!z}(\oc u))\cdot({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u))\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u))\\
\quad\leq\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot({\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u))\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+({\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1})))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(t_{2})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(t_{2}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

t={\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right)
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}={\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash\oc u\}\right)
and by
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}({\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}\right))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}({\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right))+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}({\tt der}\!\left(t^{\prime}\right)))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

t=\oc t^{\prime}
: Then
t\{x\backslash u\}=\oc(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})
and by
i.h.
on
t^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}[x\backslash\oc u])
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt P}_{\!y}(t\{x\backslash u\})&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\}))\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}\{x\backslash u\})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime}[x\backslash\oc u])\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t^{\prime})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t^{\prime}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc t^{\prime})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(\oc t^{\prime}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\oc u)\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\oc u])\end{array}

\mathtt{L}=\mathtt{L}^{\prime}[z\backslash s]
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{L}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>)\leq{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>])
. By

\alpha
-conversion,
z\notin{\tt fv}(t)
thus

{\tt P}_{\!z}(t)=0
and therefore:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>[z\backslash s])\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<t\{x\backslash u\}\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(s)\\
\quad\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\leq}\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>])+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>]))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(s)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!z}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(s)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>)+\max(1,\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!z}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(s)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(s)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot({\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!z}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(s))\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc u\right>[z\backslash s])\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(t))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc u\right>)\\
\quad=\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(t[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc u\right>])\end{array}

∎

Lemma 40
.

Let
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
, then

{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>])\succ{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)
.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathtt{L}
. Cases:

\mathtt{L}=\diamond
: Then
t=s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}]
and

u=s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}
. By induction on
s_{1}
:

s_{1}=y
: Then:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(y)\right]\uplus{\tt m}(y)\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(y))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(y)\right]\uplus{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(y)\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{2})\end{array}

We distinguish two cases:

\bullet

x=y
: Then
{\tt m}(t)=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(y)\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{2})=\left[1\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{2})
and
s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{2}
thus

{\tt m}(u)={\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})={\tt m}(s_{2})
and therefore
{\tt m}(t)\succ{\tt m}(u)
.

\bullet

x\neq y
: Then
{\tt m}(t)=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(y)\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{2})=\left[0\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{2})
and
s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=y
thus

{\tt m}(u)={\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})=\left[\,\right]

and therefore
{\tt m}(t)\succ{\tt m}(u)
.

s_{1}=\lambda y.s^{\prime}_{1}
:
We can suppose without loss of generality that
y\notin{\tt fv}(s_{2})\cup\{x\}
,
thus
s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=\lambda y.(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
.
By
i.h.
on
s^{\prime}_{1}
,
{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
.
Therefore:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((\lambda y.s^{\prime}_{1})[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(\lambda y.s^{\prime}_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(\lambda y.s^{\prime}_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(\lambda y.s^{\prime}_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s^{\prime}_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s^{\prime}_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(\lambda y.(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

s_{1}=s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2}
: Then
s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
.
By
i.h.
on
s_{1}^{1}
and
s_{1}^{2}
,

{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
and

{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
. We distinguish four cases:

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})=0
,

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})=0
,
s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{1}

and
s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{1}
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[0\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&\succ&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{1}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{1}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\geq 1
,

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})=0
and
s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{2}
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{1}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{1}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})=0
,

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\geq 1
and
s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{2}
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{1}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{1}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\geq 1
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\geq 1
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus({\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&\succ&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus({\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2}))\uplus({\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2}))\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

s_{1}=s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]
:
We can suppose without loss of generality that
y\notin{\tt fv}(s_{2})\cup\{x\}
, then

s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})[y\backslash(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})]
and by
i.h.
on
s_{1}^{1}
and
s_{1}^{2}
, one has that

{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
and

{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
. We distinguish four cases:

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})=0
,

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})=0
,
s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{1}

and
s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{2}
. Thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[0\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[0\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&\succ&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}])\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\geq 1
,

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})=0
and
s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{2}
. Then using

Lemma

38
, one has that:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\right)\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}])\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\bullet

x\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})=0
,

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\geq 1
and
s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=s_{1}^{2}
. We distinguish two cases:

\cdot

y\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
: Then

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})=0
. By

Lemma

38
,

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})=0
, thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&\succ&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\right)\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}])\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\cdot

y\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
: Then

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\geq 1
. By

Lemma

38
,

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})
, thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\\
&&\hskip 14.22636pt\uplus\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot(\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2}))\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\right)\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}])\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\bullet

x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
and
x\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{2})
: Then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\geq 1
and

{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\geq 1
. We distinguish two
cases:

\cdot

y\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
: Then

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})=0
. By

Lemma

38
,

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})
thus one has that:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
\quad={\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\right]\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
\quad\;\;\quad\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\right)\uplus\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus(\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2}))\right)\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
\quad\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
\quad={\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}])\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\cdot

y\in{\tt fv}(s_{1}^{1})
: Then

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\geq 1
. By

Lemma

38
,

{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})={\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})
thus one has that:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
\quad={\tt m}((s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}]))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\right]\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
\quad\;\;\quad\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\\
\quad\;\;\quad\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot(\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2}))\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\right)\\
\quad\;\;\quad\uplus\;{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2})\uplus(\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}^{2}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2}))\right)\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{2}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
\quad\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1})\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\cdot{\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
\quad={\tt m}(s_{1}^{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}[y\backslash s_{1}^{2}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}])\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

s_{1}={\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\right)
: Then
s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}={\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right)
and by
i.h.
on
s^{\prime}_{1}
, one has that

{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}({\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\right)[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}({\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\right))\right]\uplus{\tt m}({\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\right))\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}({\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\right)))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s^{\prime}_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s^{\prime}_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}({\tt der}\!\left(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

s_{1}=\oc s^{\prime}_{1}
: Then
s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}=\oc(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
and by
i.h.
on
s^{\prime}_{1}
, one has that

{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\succ{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})
thus:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])&=&{\tt m}((\oc s^{\prime}_{1})[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(\oc s^{\prime}_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(\oc s^{\prime}_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(\oc s^{\prime}_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s^{\prime}_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s^{\prime}_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\oc s_{2})\\
&=&{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}[x\backslash\oc s_{2}])\\
&\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}&{\tt m}(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\\
&=&{\tt m}(\oc(s^{\prime}_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}))\quad=\quad{\tt m}(s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\})\end{array}

\mathtt{L}=\mathtt{L}^{\prime}[y\backslash s]
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{L}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>])\succ{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)
. Since
y\notin{\tt fv}(s_{1})
then
{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1})=0
, and using

Lemma

39
, one therefore has
that:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>])\\
\quad={\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>[y\backslash s]])\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>[y\backslash s])\\
\quad=\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right]\uplus{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\cdot{\tt m}(s)\right)\\
\quad=\left(\left[{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1})\right]\uplus{\tt m}(s_{1})\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right)\\
\qquad\;\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right]\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad={\tt m}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>])\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right]\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad\overset{\textit{i.h.}}{\succ}{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right]\uplus\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad={\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\uplus\left[\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right]\uplus(\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>))\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad={\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>)\right]\uplus({\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1})+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!x}(s_{1}))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>))\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad={\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>])\right]\uplus({\tt P}_{\!y}(s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>]))\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad\succ{\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\uplus\left[{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\right]\uplus{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>)\cdot{\tt m}(s)\\
\quad={\tt m}(\mathtt{L}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>[y\backslash s])\end{array}

∎

Lemma 41
.

Let
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u
, then
{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(u)
.

Proof.

Let
t,u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u
. By definition, there exist a context
\mathtt{F}

and terms
s_{t},s_{u}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t=\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>
,
\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>

and
t\mapsto_{{\tt d}\oc}u
. By induction on

\mathtt{F}
:

\mathtt{F}=\diamond
: Then
t=s_{t}
,
u=s_{u}
and there exist a context
\mathtt{L}
and terms
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
s_{t}=s_{1}[y\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>]
and
s_{u}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}\{y\backslash s_{2}\}\right>
and one concludes
using
Lemma

39
.

\mathtt{F}=\lambda y.\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\lambda y.\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\lambda y.\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(u)

\mathtt{F}=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\,s
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>\,s)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)+{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)\\
\qquad\;\geq\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)+{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>\,s)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(u)\end{array}

\mathtt{F}=s\,\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s\,\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)+{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\\
\qquad\;\geq\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)+{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s\,\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(u)\end{array}

\mathtt{F}=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}[y\backslash s]
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
and

{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>[y\backslash s])\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)\\
\qquad\;\geq\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>[y\backslash s])\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(u)\end{array}

\mathtt{F}=s[y\backslash\mathtt{F}^{\prime}]
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s[y\backslash\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>])\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\\
\qquad\;\geq\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s)+\max(1,{\tt P}_{\!y}(s))\cdot{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(s[y\backslash\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>])\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)\;=\;{\tt P}_{\!x}(u)\end{array}

\mathtt{F}={\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\right)
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}({\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>\right))={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}({\tt der}\!\left(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>\right))={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(u)

\mathtt{F}=\oc\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

{\tt P}_{\!x}(t)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\oc\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\geq{\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\oc\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt P}_{\!x}(u)

∎

Lemma 42
.

Let
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u
, then
{\tt m}(t)\succ{\tt m}(u)
.

Proof.

Let
t,u\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t\rightarrow_{\mathrm{F}\!\left<{\tt s}\oc\right>}u
. By definition, there exist a context
\mathtt{F}

and terms
s_{t},s_{u}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
t=\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>
,
\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>

and
t\mapsto_{{\tt d}\oc}u
. By induction on

\mathtt{F}
:

\mathtt{F}=\diamond
: Then
t=s_{t}
,
u=s_{u}
and there exist a context
\mathtt{L}
and terms
s_{1},s_{2}\in\Lambda_{\oc}
such that
s_{t}=s_{1}[x\backslash\mathtt{L}\!\left<\oc s_{2}\right>]
and
s_{u}=\mathtt{L}\!\left<s_{1}\{x\backslash s_{2}\}\right>
and one concludes
using
Lemma

40
.

\mathtt{F}=\lambda y.\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt m}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\succ{\tt m}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:

{\tt m}(t)={\tt m}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt m}(\lambda y.\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)={\tt m}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\succ{\tt m}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt m}(\lambda y.\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt m}(\mathtt{F}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)={\tt m}(u)

\mathtt{F}=\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\,s
: By
i.h.
on

\mathtt{F}^{\prime}
, one has that

{\tt m}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{t}\right>)\succ{\tt m}(\mathtt{F}^{\prime}\!\left<s_{u}\right>)
thus:
